## Run 39208812805 (the 06:14:54Z `/oc maintainer` per-OS handoff on #656; fresh survey 06:15-06:25Z) - ONE DISPATCH: macOS test-portability fix build on #656

**State snapshot (fresh survey; main tip `70ada902d44e` via ls-remote; open PRs #698/#693/#670/#654; 31 open issues; run link https://github.com/Userfrom1995/RandomLabs/actions/runs/39208812805).**

  - **F1**: `geometry::tests::pty_pixel_fields_shape_the_geometry` (`maze-chase/crates/terminal/src/geometry.rs:257-288`) opens a pty master with no slave and asserts `TIOCSWINSZ == 0`; macOS returns `ENOTTY` -> panic (`left: -1, right: 0`). Verified in-tree: the test body matches the report exactly; the production path handles ENOTTY cleanly (test portability only). Added by `a86b3056` in #691.
  - **F2**: `Session::drop` -> `kill(); wait()` with no drain/close first; on macOS the killed child stays unreapable until the master is drained, so `plan_contract`, `tester_dynamic_layout_hostile`, and `tester_layout_goldens_live` hang forever (`sample` stacks in the report). Verified in-tree: `plan_contract.rs:221-227` shows the exact shape; the same `fn drop` pattern exists across several harnesses (headless, render_geometry, resize_race, tester_hostile, tester_render_width_hostile, tester_resize_race_hostile, tester_layout_goldens_live, tester_dynamic_layout_hostile), so the fix should harden all of them. Goldens suite added by `54c13c17` (#691); the other two suites predate the fix (`b1f16df3`, `2598ce7e`).
- **DISPATCH**: `decision.json = [{"action":"build","issue":656}]` - a scoped test-portability build: open the slave (or tolerate ENOTTY) in the geometry test; drain/close the master before reaping in every harness carrying the `Session::drop` shape; keep Linux behavior byte-identical.
